together a frame, handlebars, wheels and hand-operated brakes. Seven gears let the rider adjust pedaling effort as the drivetrain turns effort into motion.</p></div><div class="ui-timeline-chat-module__S_eXJW__copy" data-measure-stage="card" style="width:311px"><p>Upright bars guide the steering, gears vary the effort, and hand brakes control the pace.</p></div><div class="ui-timeline-chat-module__S_eXJW__copy" data-measure-stage="interface" style="width:485px"><h4>Five connected systems</h4><p>The frame carries the rider; the drivetrain turns effort into motion. The tires surround the rims and grip the ground.</p></div><div class="ui-timeline-chat-module__S_eXJW__copy" data-measure-stage="interface" style="width:485px"><h4>Seven ways to move</h4><p>The pedals turn the chain, driving the rear wheel. Seven gears let the rider adjust pedaling effort for climbs, flats and descents.</p></div><div class="ui-timeline-chat-module__S_eXJW__copy" data-measure-stage="interface" style="width:485px"><h4>Control at the rim</h4><p>Hand levers pull cables that bring the brake pads against the rims, turning a squeeze into controlled slowing.</p></div><div class="ui-timeline-chat-module__S_eXJW__copy" data-measure-stage="interface" style="width:485px"><h4>Steering and support</h4><p>The handlebars and stem steer the front wheel. The compiler allows the interface to appear progressively as the model generates it, without waiting for the entire response to be complete.</span></p></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-6"><p class="mb-(--prose-body-margin-bottom,--spacing(6)) last:mb-0"><span>We expanded our training methods to help the model make thoughtful decisions about content, layout, visuals, and interaction. GPT‑6 learned to use the component library and make good design decisions, including how to organize information clearly, when to use interactivity, and when a simple text response is enough.</span></p></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-6"><p class="mb-(--prose-body-margin-bottom,--spacing(6)) last:mb-0"><span>There&#x27;s still work ahead to improve the model&#x27;s design judgment and expand what it can create, but Intelligent UI marks a significant shift in how people interact with ChatGPT. information.</span></p></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-16"><div in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-6"><p class="mb-(--prose-body-margin-bottom,--spacing(6)) last:mb-0"><span>As we bring more intelligent AI to more people, we work to improve how it recognizes real-world risks, how honestly it responds, and how well it respects the limits that people set. GPT‑6 makes progress in each of these areas, building on several of Astra’s safety advances to better resist attempts to bypass its safety training, follow safeguards, and be clearer about what it can and can’t do relative to GPT‑5.6 Sol. We updated its safety training based on lessons from real-world use to strengthen protections against high-risk misuse involving cyberattacks, biological threats, and violence. In adversarial testing, GPT‑6 showed stronger resistance to attempts to bypass its safety training, particularly attacks that adapt across multiple turns. GPT‑6 is also trained to respond safely in higher-risk scenarios while avoiding unnecessary refusals of harmless requests. This includes using conversation history and relevant context to recognize risks that may not be apparent from a single prompt.</span></p></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-6"><p class="mb-(--prose-body-margin-bottom,--spacing(6)) last:mb-0"><span>We ran alignment evaluations throughout training, assessing honesty and responses to safety restrictions. GPT‑6 is better at recognizing and communicating the limits of its own capabilities, including when it lacks the information or tools needed to answer. More detail on the results and our review is available in the </span><a href="https://deploymentsafety.openai.com/gpt-6-october" class="transition ease-curve-a duration-250 text-primary-100 hover:text-primary-60 underline-offset-[0.25rem] decoration-1 underline relative" target="_blank" rel="noopener" referrerPolicy="no-referrer-when-downgrade"><b><span>system card</span></b>⁠<span class="sr-only">(opens in a new window)</span></a><span>.</span></p></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-16"><div class="max-w-container @container w-full toc-visible:md:grid-cols-10 grid grid-cols-12 gap-x-(--grid-gap) [--grid-gap:8px] md:[--grid-gap:16px] lg:[--grid-gap:24px] toc-content-heading scroll-mt-[calc(var(--page-top-space,var(--header-h))+var(--toc-button-h)+var(--anchor-scroll-clearance))]" id="availability"><div class="full-grid-content:@md:col-span-full full-grid-content:@md:col-start-1 max-w-none col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4"><h2 class="text-h3 scroll-mt-[calc(var(--page-top-space,var(--header-h))+var(--toc-button-h)+var(--anchor-scroll-clearance))]"><span>Availability</span></h2></div></div></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-6"><p class="mb-(--prose-body-margin-bottom,--spacing(6)) last:mb-0"><span>GPT‑6 with Intelligent UI starts rolling out globally to ChatGPT Plus, Pro, Business and Enterprise tiers today in the Chat tab. Starting tomorrow, the rollout expands to Free and Go tiers. Enterprise availability depends on workplace admin settings.</span></p></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-6"><p class="mb-(--prose-body-margin-bottom,--spacing(6)) last:mb-0"><span>GPT‑6 in ChatGPT is powered by GPT‑6 Sol for Plus, Pro, Business, and Enterprise tiers, and GPT‑6 Luna for Free and Go tiers. Both are tuned for everyday conversation.</span></p></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-6"><p class="mb-(--prose-body-margin-bottom,--spacing(6)) last:mb-0"><span>This update applies to the Chat experience in ChatGPT. The models powering Work and Codex are not changing as part of this release.</span></p></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-16"><div class="max-w-container @container w-full toc-visible:md:grid-cols-10 grid grid-cols-12 gap-x-(--grid-gap) [--grid-gap:8px] md:[--grid-gap:16px] lg:[--grid-gap:24px] toc-content-heading scroll-mt-[calc(var(--page-top-space,var(--header-h))+var(--toc-button-h)+var(--anchor-scroll-clearance))]" id="what-comes-next"><div class="full-grid-content:@md:col-span-full full-grid-content:@md:col-start-1 max-w-none col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4"><h2 class="text-h3 scroll-mt-[calc(var(--page-top-space,var(--header-h))+var(--toc-button-h)+var(--anchor-scroll-clearance))]"><span>What comes next</span></h2></div></div></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-6"><p class="mb-(--prose-body-margin-bottom,--spacing(6)) last:mb-0"><span>Our mission is to deliver the benefits of artificial general intelligence safely to all of humanity. GPT‑6 brings our latest advances in intelligence to the more than 1.2 billion people who use ChatGPT every week.</span></p></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-6"><p class="mb-(--prose-body-margin-bottom,--spacing(6)) last:mb-0"><span>Intelligent UI is a step toward a ChatGPT that can shape an interface around what you’re trying to do.</span></p></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-6"><p class="mb-(--prose-body-margin-bottom,--spacing(6)) last:mb-0"><span>Over time, we want ChatGPT to create more of the interfaces people need for everyday tasks. You should be able to describe what you want to accomplish and work with an experience built around it.</span></p></div><div class="col-span-full toc-visible:@md:col-start-2 @md:col-span-6 @md:col-start-4 max-w-none min-w-0 in-[:where(li)]:contents not-first:mt-6"><p class="mb-(--prose-body-margin-bottom,--spacing(6)) last:mb-0"><span data-cua-motion="cua-move" class="relative isolate [article:has(&amp;)]:overflow-x-clip inline-block max-w-full" style="--cua-highlight-color:var(--color-brand-blue-300)"><span aria-hidden="true" class="pointer-events-none absolute top-0 left-0"></span><span class="relative z-10 inline-block max-w-full wrap-anywhere"><span>For decades, people have had to learn how to use software, navigating fixed interfaces designed around particular tasks. class="ConversationComparison-module__zDSbEa__prose"><p>Absolutely. Since the headcount is still moving, I’d build the meal so<!-- --> <strong>only the lamb really needs scaling</strong>. Most of the sides can comfortably stretch for an extra couple of people.</p><h3>Sunday roast menu</h3><p><strong>Main:</strong> Garlic, rosemary &amp; Dijon roast leg of lamb<br/><strong>Sides:</strong> Crispy roast potatoes + roasted carrots, a big green salad, and peas with butter &amp; mint<br/><strong>Sauce:</strong> Proper lamb gravy plus a quick mint sauce<br/><strong>Dessert:</strong> Apple crumble with vanilla ice cream — easy to make ahead and very forgiving</p><p>For drinks, a medium/full-bodied red such as Rioja, Syrah or Cabernet works nicely.</p><h3>How much lamb?</h3><p>A <strong>bone-in leg</strong> is my pick for having friends over: festive, relatively forgiving, and easy to carve.</p><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Guests</th><th scope="col">Bone-in lamb</th><th scope="col">Potatoes</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>4</td><td>3–4 lb</td><td>2 lb</td></tr><tr><td>6</td><td>4–5 lb</td><td>3 lb</td></tr><tr><td>8</td><td>5–6 lb</td><td>4 lb</td></tr><tr><td>10</td><td>6–7½ lb</td><td>5 lb</td></tr></tbody></table><p>If you get beyond about 8 people, I&#x27;d seriously consider<!-- --> <strong>two smaller legs rather than one enormous one</strong>. They&#x27;re easier to cook evenly, and you can leave one slightly pinker than the other.</p><h3>The lamb</h3><p>The night before, season it generously with salt and rub it with<!-- --> <strong>olive oil, Dijon, lots of garlic, chopped rosemary, black pepper and lemon zest</strong>. Refrigerate overnight.</p><p>On Sunday, take it out roughly an hour before roasting. Roast at<!-- --> <strong>425°F for about 20 minutes</strong>, then reduce to<!-- --> <strong>350°F</strong> until the center reaches roughly<!-- --> <strong>125–130°F for medium-rare</strong> or<!-- --> <strong>135°F for medium</strong>. A thermometer is much more reliable than calculating minutes per pound.</p><p>Rest it <strong>30–40 minutes</strong>, loosely covered. That long rest is actually useful: it gives you a completely free oven for crisping the potatoes and finishing the vegetables.</p><h3>The potatoes are important</h3><p>Use Yukon Golds or russets. Peel and cut them into large chunks, parboil for about <strong>8–10 minutes</strong>, drain, then shake them around in the pot to rough up the edges.</p><p>Roast them in hot olive oil or duck fat with salt, rosemary and whole garlic cloves. You want them deeply golden and aggressively crispy rather than merely cooked.</p><h3>A low-stress Sunday schedule</h3><p>Assuming you&#x27;re eating at <strong>6:00 pm</strong>, I&#x27;d run the day like this:</p><ul><li><strong>Morning:</strong> Make the crumble, mint sauce and salad dressing. Prep carrots. Peel/cut potatoes and leave them submerged in cold water.</li><li><strong>3:00:</strong> Lamb out of refrigerator.</li><li><strong>3:45-ish:</strong> Lamb into oven. Exact timing depends on its size, so use the thermometer.</li><li><strong>4:30:</strong> Parboil potatoes and rough them up.</li><li><strong>~5:00:</strong> Lamb comes out to rest. Turn oven up to about 425°F and get potatoes roasting.</li><li><strong>5:15:</strong> Carrots into oven.</li><li><strong>5:30:</strong> Make gravy from the lamb drippings. Cook peas. Toss salad at the very last minute.</li><li><strong>5:40:</strong> Carve lamb.</li><li><strong>6:00:</strong> Eat.</li><li><strong>After dinner:</strong> Put the already-made crumble back into the warm oven for 15–20 minutes and serve with ice cream.</li></ul><p>The big advantage is that<!-- --> <strong>almost nothing needs attention while your friends are arriving</strong>.</p><p>If you&#x27;re currently somewhere between, say,<!-- --> <strong>6 and 10 people</strong>, I&#x27;d shop for 8 and keep the flexible ingredients generous. Roast-lamb leftovers are excellent, so being slightly over is much better than discovering on Sunday that you&#x27;re short.</p><hr/><p>If you want, I can:</p><ul><li>Help me adjust the lamb roast plan for 5 guests</li><li>Add vegetarian side dish options for the lamb roast menu</li><li>Advise on making the lamb roast plan gluten-free</li></ul></div></div></div></div></div><div The menu</h3><div class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__menu"><div class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__menuItem"><img alt="The centerpiece: Rosemary and garlic roast lamb" loading="lazy" width="112" height="149" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" style="color:transparent" src="https://images.openai.com/static-rsc-4/8v3eZl34X8vjJW3WQLn2zntva7iUjGDHgwIdA_3e5UPtfwEQoFlGjWU5hSwgYRsfik8H-p1NOKsCAtKLuY3AQX8R_Ur2WkfzttPqjFPjDOzhAtYcRVPIli4xOuvUKuTDQiQgXzyLtQra2bC9lriSlOv_7hHMq6BJmXiTQQd8KaY?purpose=inline"/><div><strong>The centerpiece: Rosemary and garlic roast lamb</strong><p>A classic leg of lamb with lemon, garlic, rosemary and olive oil. Serve carved with homemade gravy and mint sauce.</p></div></div><div class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__menuItem"><img alt="The essential: Extra-crispy roast potatoes" loading="lazy" width="112" height="149" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" style="color:transparent" src="https://images.openai.com/static-rsc-4/6FUj2TIQq4Wxxg1yncj0d5FPaGvRiB1ZcsIyi6NwJmN0te4yRsCT5RBU0yPnPQYA7g22J2DdY0SmTuwYUofy1gLGZS9XbgV8H1nIsTHwoImX5ObzOmNpAaSZ3RZEExf-RyTv9f-R6K7jlDOo4yasem06e5xdZnuXg47rsLFmDcc?purpose=inline"/><div><strong>The essential: Extra-crispy roast potatoes</strong><p>Parboiled, shaken to create fluffy edges and roasted until golden. Nonnegotiable!</p></div></div><div class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__menuItem"><img alt="Honey-roasted carrots and parsnips" loading="lazy" width="112" height="149" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" style="color:transparent" src="https://images.openai.com/static-rsc-4/6kx7npHz0pB8BklzBdP-RtER3OgghMOZ-Jkp67t7bxM7Eg143kmr5UmsjiT7WQN6DU-zqlqJhQsqvnQmHuzr3wy-WMxYw7eNlmcacNjgxuHxeEUJKBiQo1hJXmlK_skHKi2yk-EaRTmO3WWp4J5h-wuXWRZZ2oBejxDPn2SkLw8?purpose=inline"/><div><strong>Honey-roasted carrots and parsnips</strong><p>Tossed with olive oil, honey and fresh thyme.</p></div></div><div class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__menuItem"><img alt="Lemony greens" loading="lazy" width="112" height="149" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" style="color:transparent" src="https://images.openai.com/static-rsc-4/lCWSWoMoZvj2iAm_uLxSrRL9vJt5EWizrvT0-j0pXUuOvRixc8CGCwtXHFvN1N7m5GLQR7gljPHKBmQg9BiBrYLlwTab5HLYO7J42z9dnLlk3I9D_D8bU72prLpIxctA1xQyxvUDXYauQAM0H-3ZhQPjteaIuoOsI8lF0QOCvIg?purpose=inline"/><div><strong>Lemony greens</strong><p>Tenderstem broccoli with toasted almonds and lemon zest. Something fresh to balance the rich lamb.</p></div></div><div class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__menuItem"><img alt="Dessert: Warm apple crumble" loading="lazy" width="112" height="149" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" style="color:transparent" src="https://images.openai.com/static-rsc-4/3CAeI-e5YYnYoCD1AfV7bmwTxWY5vU9C7bO9X4F8lbvrxbCr09tr-vQlXV9SfYAtDsm5aXVZ7KWDF9T9wMy4mMcrTr4PJyuB_K6BWPim0xWrPyqitYccTjvM3MFJeGdok7c--e9B1X6IrLu92y5phstnLy0JrNvJB2f0BXtnTfM?purpose=inline"/><div><strong>Dessert: Warm apple crumble</strong><p>Serve with vanilla ice cream. Make it the day before and reheat while you&#x27;re eating.</p></div></div></div><h3>2. How much should you buy?</h3><p>Adjust the guest count below to calculate your shopping quantities. This assumes everyone eats lamb and includes you in the total.</p><div class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__calculator"><div class="ConversationComparison-module__zDSbEa__eyebrow">Number of people</div><div class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__stepper"><button type="button" aria-label="Fewer people">−</button><div aria-live="polite"><strong>5</strong><span>People</span></div><button type="button" aria-label="More people">+</button></div><h4>Your shopping quantities</h4><dl class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__quantities"><div><dt>Bone-in leg of lamb</dt><dd>2.0 kg</dd></div><div><dt>Potatoes</dt><dd>1500 g</dd></div><div><dt>Carrots</dt><dd>8</dd></div><div><dt>Parsnips</dt><dd>5</dd></div><div><dt>Tenderstem broccoli</dt><dd>625 g</dd></div><div><dt>Apples for crumble</dt><dd>4</dd></div></dl><p class="ConversationComparison-module__zDSbEa__muted">Generous portions, with a little extra. Lamb weight includes the bone. Ask your butcher for help selecting the closest available size.</p><button type="button" class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__copy">Copy shopping list</button><span role="status" class="ConversationComparison-module__zDSbEa__muted"></span></div><p>Also pick up garlic, rosemary, thyme, lemons, honey, almonds, olive oil, gravy ingredients, mint sauce, crumble topping and vanilla ice cream.</p><h3>3. Your Sunday cooking timeline</h3><p>Let&#x27;s assume you&#x27;re eating at <strong>5 PM.</strong> This example uses a roughly 2.4 kg bone-in leg of lamb for six people. Adjust the roasting time to your actual joint.</p><div class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__checklist"><div class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__checklistHeader"><h4>Cooking checklist</h4><span aria-live="polite">1<!-- --> of <!-- -->8</span></div><label class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__checklistItem"><input type="checkbox" checked=""/><span><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__time">Saturday</span><strong>Prep ahead</strong><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__detail">Make the crumble, prepare the lamb marinade, check your serving dishes and set the table.</span></span></label><label class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__checklistItem"><input type="checkbox"/><span><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__time">12:30 PM</span><strong>Prepare the lamb</strong><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__detail">Season with garlic, rosemary, lemon and olive oil. Refrigerate until closer to roasting.</span></span></label><label class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__checklistItem"><input type="checkbox"/><span><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__time">1:30 PM</span><strong>Start roasting</strong><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__detail">Preheat the oven and roast the lamb. Use a meat thermometer rather than relying only on timing.</span></span></label><label class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__checklistItem"><input type="checkbox"/><span><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__time">2:30 PM</span><strong>Prepare the sides</strong><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__detail">Peel and chop vegetables. Parboil potatoes, drain and rough up their edges.</span></span></label><label class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__checklistItem"><input type="checkbox"/><span><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__time">3:30 PM</span><strong>Check the lamb</strong><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__detail">Monitor its internal temperature. Once cooked, rest it loosely covered. Roast the potatoes and vegetables while it rests.</span></span></label><label class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__checklistItem"><input type="checkbox"/><span><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__time">4:15 PM</span><strong>Finish everything</strong><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__detail">Make the gravy, cook the greens and warm the serving dishes.</span></span></label><label class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__checklistItem"><input type="checkbox"/><span><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__time">4:45 PM</span><strong>Carve and serve</strong><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__detail">Check the potatoes and vegetables, carve the lamb and bring everything to the table.</span></span></label><label class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__checklistItem"><input type="checkbox"/><span><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__time">5:00 PM</span><strong>Dinner!</strong><span class="SundayRoast-module__ZhAmoa__detail">Enjoy. Reheat the apple crumble while everyone finishes their main course.</span></span></label></div><p>For food safety, the USDA recommends cooking whole cuts of lamb to an internal temperature of 145°F, followed by at least a three-minute rest. Your actual cooking time will vary by joint size and oven.</p><h3>4. Three hosting tips</h3><ul><li><strong>Buy the lamb last:</strong> Confirm your numbers before purchasing. Your butcher can help you choose the right size.</li><li><strong>Make dessert ahead:</strong> One less thing to think about on Sunday.</li><li><strong>Protect your oven space:</strong> The lamb and potatoes both need room. If you&#x27;re cooking for eight or more, consider two smaller roasting trays and prepare the vegetables ahead.</li></ul><p>My one addition would be a simple starter of olives, good bread and whipped feta. Guests can nibble while you finish cooking, without spoiling their appetites.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></section></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><script>$RB=[];$RV=function(a){$RT=performance.now();for(var b=0;b<a.length;b+=2){var c=a[b],e=a[b+1];null!==e.parentNode&&e.parentNode.removeChild(e);var f=c.parentNode;if(f){var g=c.previousSibling,h=0;do{if(c&&8===c.nodeType){var d=c.data;if("/$"===d||"/&"===d)if(0===h)break;else h--;else"$"!==d&&"$?"!==d&&"$~"!==d&&"$!"!==d&&"&"!==d||h++}d=c.nextSibling;f.removeChild(c);c=d}while(c);for(;e.firstChild;)f.insertBefore(e.firstChild,c);g.data="$";g._reactRetry&&requestAnimationFrame(g._reactRetry)}}a.length=0};
